display="inline-block" overflow="visible" style="vertical-align:text-bottom"><path d="M8 9a1.5 1.5 0 1 0 0-3 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 0 3ZM1.5 9a1.5 1.5 0 1 0 0-3 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 0 3Zm13 0a1.5 1.5 0 1 0 0-3 1.5 1.5 0 0 0 0 3Z"></path></svg></button><span class="prc-TooltipV2-Tooltip-tLeuB" data-direction="s" data-component="Tooltip" aria-hidden="true" id="_R_fqlmkjmr9mm5_">Issue body actions</span></div></div></div></div></div><div class="IssueBody__1iGjHw" id="issue-body-viewer" data-testid="issue-body-viewer"><div data-testid="markdown-body" data-team-hovercards-enabled="true" class="markdown-body" data-turbolinks="false"><div class="markdown-body safe-html-box__Uwc6Al"><p dir="auto">Hi, I am developing a Quarkus reactive component that consumes a record from Kafka and then makes an HTTP invocation via SOAP.</p>
<p dir="auto">I generated the client with wsdl2java provided by Quarkus and I am trying to use the async methods from the generated client.<br>
I set the receive timeout on the client, but no failures are fired when the timeout expires. The client always waits for the response from the server.</p>
<p dir="auto">While I was looking for a solution in source code I may have spotted the snippet that produces that behaviour.<br>
In class VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.RequestBodyHandler.Mode.Async the awaitResponse method is empty. If I am in sync mode, this method detects timeout expiration and fires a SocketTimeoutException instead.</p>
<p dir="auto">Is there a way to make quarkus-cxf to detect the receive timeout expiration without bocking in the event loop?</p></div></div><div class="IssueBodyTaskList__-FlQre"><div role="toolbar" aria-label="Reactions" class="d-flex gap-1 flex-wrap"></div></div></div></div></div></div></div></div><!--$--><div data-testid="issue-viewer-comments-container" class="react-comments-container"><div
